- **vc-implement**: End-to-end implementation skill for when the user is done talking and needs the thing built. VC-ship WRITE stage: full delivery with autonomous decision making. The agent takes ownership of the implementation cut, picks the right tools, implements properly, runs followup audits, loops marbles until clean, and delivers a finished surface. No phase theatre; no permission-seeking on obvious moves. The user says what; the agent figures out how. - **vdd-explain**: All lookup intent about existing code/features/libraries/structure/conventions.
